Entry 1

A: Yes, pets are welcomed and the address is 8669 West Sahara Avenue.

A: how about Budget Suites of America with a 3.4 rating?

U: Great, let's make a booking.
A: When would you expect to check out?
U: check out on the 14th
A: great and your check in is still March 8th?
U: No, the 9th of March please.
A: please confirm, reserving 1 room at Hampton Inn Las Vegas/Summerlin in Las Vegas with a check in on march 9th and check out on March 14th.
U: perfect
A: great, you are confirmed
U: thank you.good bye
A: good bye.

Entry 2
U: Hello! Can you help me find bus tickets?
A: I'd be happy to help. Where will you be departing from and traveling to? Do you have a preference on time?
U: Yes, I'd like to leave SD in the afternoon 3:45 and travel to Long Beach.
A: Which date will you be leaving and how many in your party?

A: You need 3 tickets for March 11th on the bus leaving San Diego at 3:45 pm and going to Long Beach. I have found an Economy ticket. Is this correct?
U: Can we change that to the bus that is departing at quarter past 2 in the afternoon? Also, I'd like Flexible tickets, if possible.
A: Let me review those changes. You'd like 3 Flexible tickets for the 2:15 pm. Is this correct?
U: No, I'm sorry. I need to change my arrangements. I need a bus for two to LAX.
A: You need 2 tickets to Los Angeles. Is this correct?
U: Yes, I'm sorry for the confusion. Can you tell me the name of the bus station that I will be departing from?
A: It is the Santa Fe Depot. Unfortunately, your ticket purchase has been unsuccessful. There is a bus leaving at 2:10 pm with a ticket price of $42. Would that work for you?
U: Yes, that will be fine.
A: Your tickets have been confirmed.
U: Can you help me find a hotel in Los Angeles?
A: There are 10 that may work for you. May I recommend the Ac Hotel By Marriott Beverly Hills? It is nearby and has a 4.4 rating.
U: That would be great. I'll need 3 rooms checking in on the 11th of March and leaving on the 14th of March.
A: You'd like 3 rooms at the Ac Hotel By Marriott Beverly Hills in Los Angeles for an arrival of March 11th. You will be checking-out on March 14th. Is this correct?
U: Yes, that's right.
A: Great! Your reservation has been successful. You will be receiving an email confirmation shortly.
U: Perfect. Thanks for the help. That will be all.
A: You're welcome! Enjoy your trip.
